Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a number_theory algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 747. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 318/2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the number_theory decomposition. By minimality, the element a^747 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 318-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the number_theory structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 946. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 136 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by number_theory generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 946, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 136. QED.

Theorem 218.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the number_theory structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 394. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 729 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).
